# UK English Pass 題庫教師審閱版

> 2026.11 重新製作原創版本。

## b1-01 · A rainy street festival

**B1 / 節日**

- **Maya:** The street festival starts at eleven, but the forecast says heavy rain after two.
- **Tom:** Then let’s arrive early. I mainly want to see the lion dance at half past eleven.
- **Maya:** Good idea. We can have lunch at the community hall if the outdoor tables are wet.
- **Tom:** I’ll bring a light raincoat. The organisers say umbrellas block people’s view.

1. What does Tom most want to see?
   - A. The lion dance / B. The food market / C. A fireworks show
   - **答案：A** — Tom 明確說他主要想看十一時半的舞獅。
2. Why will they arrive early?
   - A. To avoid an afternoon downpour / B. To help set up tables / C. To buy cheaper tickets
   - **答案：A** — 預報下午二時後有大雨，所以他們決定早到。
3. Which item is more suitable than an umbrella?
   - A. A light raincoat / B. A picnic blanket / C. A large hat
   - **答案：A** — 主辦方表示雨傘會阻礙視線，因此 Tom 會帶輕便雨衣。

---

## b1-02 · Lantern evening plans

**B1 / 節日**

- **Nina:** Are you still coming to the lantern evening on Saturday?
- **George:** Yes, but I finish work at six. Does the parade begin straight away?
- **Nina:** No, the craft stalls open at five, and the parade leaves the library at seven fifteen.
- **George:** Perfect. I’ll meet you by the library steps at seven. I made my lantern from an old cereal box.

1. When will George meet Nina?
   - A. At 7:00 / B. At 6:00 / C. At 7:15
   - **答案：A** — George 說會在七時於圖書館梯級旁會合。
2. What happens at 7:15?
   - A. The parade leaves the library / B. The craft stalls open / C. George finishes work
   - **答案：A** — Nina 說巡遊於七時十五分從圖書館出發。
3. What is special about George’s lantern?
   - A. It uses recycled packaging / B. It was bought at work / C. It has no light
   - **答案：A** — 燈籠由舊穀物早餐盒製成，屬重用物料。

---

## b1-03 · The cheaper train

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Sam:** The direct train to Brighton is thirty-eight pounds. That’s more than I expected.
- **Priya:** There’s a slower one for twenty-four pounds, but we change at Hove.
- **Sam:** It only takes twenty minutes longer. I’d rather save the money for lunch.
- **Priya:** Agreed. Let’s take the 9:10 and sit near the doors so the change is easy.

1. Which train do they choose?
   - A. The 9:10 slower train / B. The direct train / C. The evening train
   - **答案：A** — 他們同意乘搭 9:10、需要轉車但較便宜的班次。
2. Why does Sam prefer it?
   - A. To save money for lunch / B. To arrive before nine / C. To avoid Hove
   - **答案：A** — Sam 寧願把省下的錢用於午餐。
3. How much longer does it take?
   - A. Twenty minutes / B. Thirty-eight minutes / C. Twenty-four minutes
   - **答案：A** — 對話直接指出只多花二十分鐘。

---

## b1-04 · Cycling to the market

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Ellie:** Shall we cycle to the farmers’ market? The new riverside path is open.
- **Ben:** I’d like to, but my back tyre feels soft. The bus might be safer today.
- **Ellie:** There’s a repair stand beside the park entrance. We could pump it up there.
- **Ben:** All right. If it still loses air, we’ll lock the bikes and catch the number twelve bus.

1. What problem does Ben have?
   - A. A soft back tyre / B. A broken lock / C. A missing bus pass
   - **答案：A** — Ben 說後輪胎氣不足。
2. What will they try first?
   - A. Use the repair stand / B. Catch the number twelve bus / C. Walk beside the river
   - **答案：A** — 他們先到公園入口旁的維修站打氣。
3. When will they take the bus?
   - A. If the tyre keeps losing air / B. If the market is busy / C. If the path is closed
   - **答案：A** — 若輪胎仍然漏氣，他們才鎖車轉乘巴士。

---

## b1-05 · A surprise retirement tea

**B1 / 特別場合**

- **Aisha:** We need to keep Mrs Evans in the office until four for her surprise tea.
- **Luke:** I can ask her to check my report at half past three. How long do you need?
- **Aisha:** About twenty minutes. The cake is arriving at ten to four through the side door.
- **Luke:** Fine. I’ll bring her to the staff room when you send me a message.

1. Why will Luke ask about his report?
   - A. To delay Mrs Evans / B. To improve it before lunch / C. To invite her outside
   - **答案：A** — 查報告只是讓 Mrs Evans 留在辦公室的安排。
2. When is the cake due?
   - A. 3:50 / B. 3:30 / C. 4:20
   - **答案：A** — ten to four 即下午三時五十分。
3. What will Aisha do when ready?
   - A. Message Luke / B. Call the baker / C. Open the main door
   - **答案：A** — Aisha 準備好後會傳訊息給 Luke。

---

## b1-06 · Birthday picnic change

**B1 / 特別場合**

- **Ruby:** The park café rang. They can’t prepare our birthday picnic because their fridge has stopped working.
- **Callum:** That’s a shame. We could each bring something instead. I can make sandwiches.
- **Ruby:** Great. I’ll bring fruit and ask Leila to bring drinks. We still have the picnic tables booked.
- **Callum:** Let’s text everyone tonight so nobody buys food at the café tomorrow.

1. Why can the café not prepare the picnic?
   - A. Its fridge is broken / B. The tables are fully booked / C. The staff are at a party
   - **答案：A** — 咖啡店的雪櫃壞了，不能準備食物。
2. What will Callum make?
   - A. Sandwiches / B. Fruit salad / C. Drinks
   - **答案：A** — Callum 主動提出製作三文治。
3. Why will they text everyone tonight?
   - A. To stop people buying café food / B. To cancel the birthday / C. To change the park
   - **答案：A** — 他們要及早告知大家自行帶食物。

---

## b1-07 · Choosing a film

**B1 / 娛樂**

- **Holly:** The comedy starts at eight, but it finishes after ten thirty. That’s late for a weekday.
- **Dan:** What about the nature documentary at seven? Reviews say the photography is excellent.
- **Holly:** I saw it last month. There’s also a mystery at quarter past seven.
- **Dan:** Let’s choose that. We’ll have time for the last bus home.

1. Which film do they choose?
   - A. The mystery / B. The comedy / C. The documentary
   - **答案：A** — 他們最後決定看七時十五分的懸疑片。
2. Why not choose the comedy?
   - A. It finishes too late / B. Its reviews are poor / C. Dan has seen it
   - **答案：A** — 喜劇在平日晚上十時半後才完場，對他們太遲。
3. What practical concern affects the choice?
   - A. Catching the last bus / B. Finding cheap popcorn / C. Booking front seats
   - **答案：A** — Dan 提到這樣有時間趕上尾班巴士。

---

## b1-08 · Museum late opening

**B1 / 娛樂**

- **Ivy:** The science museum stays open until nine on Fridays, and entry after six is half price.
- **Noah:** Brilliant. Is the space talk included in the ticket?
- **Ivy:** Yes, but places are limited. We should reserve online before we go.
- **Noah:** I’ll book it now. Let’s meet inside because the forecast is cold.

1. What is cheaper after six?
   - A. Museum entry / B. The space talk only / C. Online booking
   - **答案：A** — 星期五六時後入場費半價。
2. What must they reserve?
   - A. A place at the space talk / B. A table in the café / C. A coat locker
   - **答案：A** — 太空講座座位有限，需網上預留。
3. Why meet inside?
   - A. The weather will be cold / B. The entrance is hard to find / C. Noah will arrive late
   - **答案：A** — Noah 因預報寒冷而建議在館內會面。

---

## b1-09 · First guitar lesson

**B1 / 音樂**

- **Zoe:** How was your first guitar lesson?
- **Martin:** Better than I expected. I couldn’t change chords quickly, but the teacher was patient.
- **Zoe:** Did you learn a whole song?
- **Martin:** Only the introduction. I’m practising ten minutes every morning instead of one long session at weekends.

1. What was difficult for Martin?
   - A. Changing chords quickly / B. Reading the song title / C. Holding the guitar
   - **答案：A** — Martin 說自己未能快速轉和弦。
2. How much of the song did he learn?
   - A. Only the introduction / B. The whole song / C. The final verse
   - **答案：A** — 他只學會歌曲的引子。
3. What is his new practice plan?
   - A. Ten minutes each morning / B. One hour every Saturday / C. Only during lessons
   - **答案：A** — 他會每天早上練十分鐘。

---

## b1-10 · Community choir audition

**B1 / 音樂**

- **Owen:** I’m thinking of joining the community choir, but the word “audition” makes me nervous.
- **Fatima:** It isn’t a competition. The conductor only listens to find which group suits your voice.
- **Owen:** That sounds less frightening. Do I need to prepare a song?
- **Fatima:** No. You’ll sing a few notes with the piano, then join Thursday’s rehearsal if you want.

1. Why does the conductor listen to new singers?
   - A. To choose the right voice group / B. To select one competition winner / C. To record an album
   - **答案：A** — 試音目的是判斷聲部分組，並非比賽。
2. Does Owen need to prepare a song?
   - A. No / B. Yes, two songs / C. The conversation does not say
   - **答案：A** — Fatima 明確回答不需要。
3. What can Owen attend afterwards?
   - A. Thursday’s rehearsal / B. A piano exam / C. A Saturday concert
   - **答案：A** — 完成簡單試音後可參加星期四排練。

---

## b1-11 · A day at the coast

**B1 / 近期個人經歷**

- **Grace:** You went to Whitby yesterday, didn’t you?
- **Imran:** Yes. The morning was foggy, so we visited the abbey first instead of walking on the beach.
- **Grace:** Did the weather improve?
- **Imran:** By lunchtime it was sunny. We walked along the harbour, but the boat trips were cancelled because the sea was rough.

1. Where did Imran go first?
   - A. The abbey / B. The beach / C. The harbour
   - **答案：A** — 因早上有霧，他們先參觀修道院。
2. What improved by lunchtime?
   - A. The weather / B. The sea conditions / C. The boat timetable
   - **答案：A** — 到午飯時天氣轉晴。
3. Why were boat trips cancelled?
   - A. The sea was rough / B. The harbour was closed / C. There were too few tourists
   - **答案：A** — 海面大浪令船程取消。

---

## b1-12 · The cooking workshop

**B1 / 近期個人經歷**

- **Leo:** I tried a bread-making workshop on Sunday. My first loaf looked terrible.
- **Mei:** But did it taste all right?
- **Leo:** Surprisingly, yes. I added too much flour while shaping it, so it was rather heavy.
- **Mei:** Will you try again?
- **Leo:** Definitely. Next time I’ll leave the dough sticky and wait longer for it to rise.

1. What was wrong with Leo’s first loaf?
   - A. It was heavy / B. It tasted terrible / C. It was too sticky
   - **答案：A** — 加了太多麵粉令麵包質地較重。
2. How did it taste?
   - A. Better than its appearance suggested / B. Exactly as bad as it looked / C. The conversation does not say
   - **答案：A** — Leo 用 surprisingly 表示味道出乎意料地可以。
3. What will he change next time?
   - A. Use less flour and allow more rising time / B. Shape it for longer / C. Bake it on Saturday
   - **答案：A** — 他會保留麵糰黏性並等待更久發酵。

---

## b1-13 · Talking about gardening

**B1 / 自選主題討論**

- **Tutor:** You chose balcony gardening as your topic. What attracted you to it?
- **Sophie:** I wanted fresh herbs, but I only had a small outdoor space.
- **Tutor:** What did you learn from your first attempt?
- **Sophie:** That sunlight matters more than pot size. My mint grew well, but the tomatoes needed a brighter corner.

1. Why did Sophie begin balcony gardening?
   - A. To grow fresh herbs / B. To win a local prize / C. To fill a large garden
   - **答案：A** — 她想種新鮮香草，且只有小型戶外空間。
2. What mattered more than pot size?
   - A. Sunlight / B. Plant labels / C. Water temperature
   - **答案：A** — Sophie 明確說陽光比花盆大小更重要。
3. Which plant needed a brighter place?
   - A. Tomatoes / B. Mint / C. Herbs in general
   - **答案：A** — 番茄需要放在光線更充足的角落。

---

## b1-14 · Photography as a hobby

**B1 / 自選主題討論**

- **Tutor:** Why do you prefer taking photographs early in the morning?
- **Kieran:** The streets are quieter and the light is softer. I notice details that I miss later.
- **Tutor:** Do you use an expensive camera?
- **Kieran:** No, usually my phone. Planning where to stand makes a bigger difference than buying new equipment.

1. Why does Kieran photograph early?
   - A. The streets are quieter and light softer / B. His phone only works then / C. Shops offer discounts
   - **答案：A** — 他指出早上街道較靜、光線較柔和。
2. What camera does he usually use?
   - A. His phone / B. An expensive studio camera / C. A borrowed film camera
   - **答案：A** — 他通常用手機拍攝。
3. What does he consider most important?
   - A. Choosing where to stand / B. Buying new equipment / C. Editing every photograph
   - **答案：A** — 他認為構圖位置比新器材影響更大。

---

## b1-15 · Understanding a follow-up

**B1 / 理解提問**

- **Examiner:** Tell me about a journey you enjoyed.
- **Ana:** I took a train through Wales last summer. The mountain views were beautiful.
- **Examiner:** Had you expected the journey itself to be memorable?
- **Ana:** Not really. I chose the train because it was cheaper than flying, so the scenery was an unexpected bonus.

1. What is the examiner asking in the follow-up?
   - A. Whether Ana thought the journey would be special / B. Whether Ana had visited Wales before / C. Why flying was beautiful
   - **答案：A** — follow-up 查問她事前是否預期旅程本身令人難忘。
2. Why did Ana originally choose the train?
   - A. It was cheaper than flying / B. She wanted mountain views / C. The airport was closed
   - **答案：A** — 她原先因火車較便宜而選擇它。
3. How did Ana feel about the scenery?
   - A. Pleasantly surprised / B. Disappointed / C. Uninterested
   - **答案：A** — unexpected bonus 表示意外驚喜。

---

## b1-16 · Clarifying the question

**B1 / 理解提問**

- **Examiner:** What sort of live entertainment is available where you live?
- **Jay:** Do you mean performances that I can attend in person?
- **Examiner:** Yes, exactly.
- **Jay:** There is a small theatre and free music in the square during summer. The theatre is more varied, but the outdoor concerts feel friendlier.

1. Why does Jay ask a question?
   - A. To check the meaning of “live entertainment” / B. To avoid giving an answer / C. To ask for a ticket
   - **答案：A** — Jay 用反問確認 live entertainment 是否指現場演出。
2. What is free in summer?
   - A. Music in the square / B. Every theatre performance / C. Cinema tickets
   - **答案：A** — 廣場夏季有免費音樂演出。
3. Which place offers more variety?
   - A. The small theatre / B. The square / C. The conversation does not say
   - **答案：A** — Jay 說劇院節目較多元。

---

## b1-17 · Library announcement

**B1 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Announcer:** Attention please. The second floor will close at four today while new shelves are installed.
- **Visitor:** Excuse me, can I still return books after four?
- **Announcer:** Yes. The ground-floor desk stays open until six. Computers will be available, but printing may be interrupted for about fifteen minutes.
- **Visitor:** Thank you. I’ll print my form now, then return my books later.

1. Which area closes at four?
   - A. The second floor / B. The ground-floor desk / C. The whole library
   - **答案：A** — 廣播只說二樓四時關閉。
2. What may stop briefly?
   - A. Printing / B. Book returns / C. Computer use
   - **答案：A** — 打印服務可能中斷約十五分鐘。
3. What will the visitor do first?
   - A. Print a form / B. Return books / C. Install shelves
   - **答案：A** — 訪客說先打印表格，稍後才還書。

---

## b1-18 · Café order correction

**B1 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Server:** Here’s your chicken sandwich and black coffee.
- **Customer:** Sorry, I ordered the cheese sandwich. The coffee is right, though.
- **Server:** My apologies. I’ll replace the sandwich. Would you like the salad without onions as requested?
- **Customer:** Yes, please. I’m not allergic; I just dislike raw onion.

1. What part of the order is wrong?
   - A. The sandwich / B. The coffee / C. The salad dressing
   - **答案：A** — 客人點芝士三文治，但送來雞肉三文治。
2. Which request is still correct?
   - A. No onions in the salad / B. No coffee / C. Extra chicken
   - **答案：A** — 客人確認沙律不要洋蔥。
3. Why does the customer avoid raw onion?
   - A. Personal preference / B. An allergy / C. Medical advice
   - **答案：A** — 客人清楚說並非敏感，只是不喜歡。

---

## b1-19 · Sports centre message

**B1 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Receptionist:** Hello, this is Westfield Sports Centre. Tonight’s beginner swimming class has moved from the main pool to the training pool.
- **Dylan:** Is it still at half past seven?
- **Receptionist:** Yes, but please arrive ten minutes early because the training pool entrance is behind the building.
- **Dylan:** Thanks. I’ll allow extra time to find it.

1. What has changed?
   - A. The pool used for the class / B. The class time / C. The skill level
   - **答案：A** — 課堂由主池移至訓練池。
2. What time does the class begin?
   - A. 7:30 / B. 7:20 / C. 8:00
   - **答案：A** — 課堂仍於七時半開始；七時二十分是建議到達時間。
3. Why should Dylan arrive early?
   - A. The entrance is in a different place / B. He must pay again / C. The lesson is longer
   - **答案：A** — 訓練池入口在建築物後方，需預留尋找時間。

---

## b1-20 · Changing a volunteer shift

**B1 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Coordinator:** Could you help at the charity shop on Friday morning instead of Thursday afternoon?
- **Amelia:** I have a dentist appointment at ten on Friday, but I could come from eleven thirty.
- **Coordinator:** That works. We mainly need help sorting donated books before the lunchtime delivery.
- **Amelia:** I’ll come straight from the dentist and stay until two.

1. Why can Amelia not start early on Friday?
   - A. She has a dentist appointment / B. She is working Thursday / C. She must collect books
   - **答案：A** — 她星期五十時要看牙醫。
2. What task needs doing?
   - A. Sorting donated books / B. Delivering lunch / C. Cleaning the dentist’s office
   - **答案：A** — 店舖需要人整理捐贈書籍。
3. How long will Amelia stay?
   - A. Until 2:00 / B. Until 11:30 / C. Until lunchtime only
   - **答案：A** — 她會由十一時半做到下午二時。
