## b2-01 · Rethinking homework

**B2 / 教育**

- **Ms Cole:** I’m considering replacing Friday worksheets with short research tasks. Pupils complete the sheets, but rarely discuss them afterwards.
- **Mr Shah:** Research could be more engaging, although some families have limited internet access.
- **Ms Cole:** True. We could let pupils choose between online research and interviewing someone at home.
- **Mr Shah:** That keeps the purpose while making the task fairer. We should trial it with one class before changing the whole year group.

1. What weakness does Ms Cole see in worksheets?
   - A. They do not lead to much discussion / B. They require internet access / C. They take a whole week
   - **答案：A** — 學生雖完成工作紙，之後卻很少討論。
2. Why is an interview option suggested?
   - A. To make access fairer / B. To assess speaking only / C. To reduce teacher marking
   - **答案：A** — 考慮到部分家庭上網受限，提供訪問選項較公平。
3. What does Mr Shah recommend first?
   - A. A trial with one class / B. Immediate change for all pupils / C. Cancelling Friday homework
   - **答案：A** — 他建議先在一班試行。

---

## b2-02 · Choosing a college course

**B2 / 教育**

- **Adviser:** Your application mentions both graphic design and marketing. Which matters more to you?
- **Rosa:** I enjoy design, but I also want to understand why campaigns succeed. The combined course seems practical, though it includes fewer studio hours.
- **Adviser:** That trade-off is important. You could take the marketing course and join the design society.
- **Rosa:** Perhaps, but I learn best through projects. I think the combined course fits that style, even if I practise drawing independently.

1. What is the disadvantage of the combined course?
   - A. Fewer studio hours / B. No practical projects / C. No marketing content
   - **答案：A** — Rosa 指出綜合課程的工作室時數較少。
2. Why does Rosa still favour it?
   - A. Its project-based approach suits her / B. It guarantees a job / C. It avoids independent practice
   - **答案：A** — 她表示自己透過項目學得最好。
3. What is the adviser’s alternative?
   - A. Study marketing and join a design society / B. Study drawing abroad / C. Leave college for work
   - **答案：A** — 顧問建議主修市場推廣並參加設計學會。

---

## b2-03 · Guests and punctuality

**B2 / 國家習俗**

- **Harriet:** When I first moved here, I arrived exactly on time for every dinner invitation and sometimes found the host still cooking.
- **Kenji:** Where I grew up, arriving late can seem disrespectful, so I understand why you did that.
- **Harriet:** Now I ask what people mean by “come around seven”. With close friends, it often means any time before half past.
- **Kenji:** That’s sensible. Customs are useful guides, but asking prevents us from treating every household as identical.

1. What surprised Harriet at first?
   - A. Hosts were sometimes not ready at the stated time / B. Guests always ate before seven / C. Dinner invitations were rare
   - **答案：A** — 她準時到達時，有時主人仍在煮食。
2. What does “around seven” often mean among close friends?
   - A. A flexible arrival before 7:30 / B. Exactly 7:00 / C. After 8:00
   - **答案：A** — 她說親友間通常指七時至七時半前。
3. What broader view does Kenji express?
   - A. Ask rather than assume every family follows one custom / B. Customs should never change / C. Punctuality is always disrespectful
   - **答案：A** — Kenji 認為習俗只是指引，直接詢問可避免一概而論。

---

## b2-04 · Sharing food at New Year

**B2 / 國家習俗**

- **Leah:** My neighbours invited me to their New Year meal and asked everyone to bring a dish connected with home.
- **Arun:** That sounds welcoming, but did choosing one feel difficult?
- **Leah:** Yes. My family has moved several times, so “home” is not a single place. I made my grandmother’s spiced bread but used local apples.
- **Arun:** Then the dish tells your actual story rather than pretending traditions never change.

1. Why was choosing a dish difficult for Leah?
   - A. She associates home with several places / B. She cannot cook bread / C. Her neighbours dislike apples
   - **答案：A** — 她的家庭多次搬遷，「家」並非單一地方。
2. How did Leah adapt the family recipe?
   - A. She added local apples / B. She removed all spices / C. She bought it ready-made
   - **答案：A** — 她用本地蘋果配祖母的香料麵包食譜。
3. How does Arun interpret the dish?
   - A. As a truthful mix of experience and tradition / B. As a failed traditional recipe / C. As proof customs must stay unchanged
   - **答案：A** — Arun 認為這道菜反映她真正的經歷，顯示傳統會演變。

---

## b2-05 · Moving out of the city

**B2 / 鄉村與城市生活**

- **Ethan:** Since moving to the village, I sleep better and know more of my neighbours. Still, I miss being able to walk to a late-night chemist.
- **Mariam:** Could improved bus services solve that?
- **Ethan:** Partly, but the last bus leaves at nine. A shared electric car scheme might help people who only need a car occasionally.
- **Mariam:** It could, provided membership is affordable. Otherwise it would serve commuters like you while excluding older residents.

1. What city convenience does Ethan miss?
   - A. A late-night chemist within walking distance / B. Knowing his neighbours / C. Quiet nights
   - **答案：A** — 他懷念可步行到深夜藥房。
2. Why are buses only a partial solution?
   - A. The last service is early / B. They are electric / C. They only serve older residents
   - **答案：A** — 尾班巴士九時開出，未能滿足較晚需要。
3. What condition does Mariam place on car sharing?
   - A. Membership must be affordable / B. Only commuters may join / C. Cars must replace all buses
   - **答案：A** — 她擔心價格太高會排除長者。

---

## b2-06 · A quieter city centre

**B2 / 鄉村與城市生活**

- **Planner:** The council proposes removing through-traffic from Market Street and adding benches and trees.
- **Resident:** I support cleaner air, but shop owners worry customers will go elsewhere if parking disappears.
- **Planner:** The plan keeps short-stay spaces on side roads and increases the shuttle bus to every ten minutes.
- **Resident:** That helps, though the council should publish sales figures during the trial, not just traffic data.

1. What will be removed from Market Street?
   - A. Through-traffic / B. All shops / C. Shuttle buses
   - **答案：A** — 計劃移除穿越 Market Street 的車流。
2. How does the plan address parking concerns?
   - A. It keeps short-stay spaces nearby / B. It builds an underground car park / C. It gives every shop a garage
   - **答案：A** — 附近支路會保留短時泊車位。
3. What extra evidence does the resident want?
   - A. Shop sales figures / B. Tree heights / C. Bus-driver interviews only
   - **答案：A** — 居民希望試行期間亦公布銷售數據。

---

## b2-07 · The value of local wool

**B2 / 本地產品**

- **Shopkeeper:** Customers like the idea of local wool, but some hesitate when they see the price.
- **Designer:** Small batches cost more to process. We could explain that the farmers are paid fairly and repairs are free for two years.
- **Shopkeeper:** A label would help, but I don’t want vague claims about sustainability.
- **Designer:** Then include the farm, mill and transport distance for each item. Specific facts are more convincing than a green symbol.

1. Why is the wool relatively expensive?
   - A. It is processed in small batches / B. It travels very far / C. Repairs cost extra
   - **答案：A** — 小批量加工令成本較高。
2. What concern does the shopkeeper have about labels?
   - A. Environmental claims may be vague / B. Customers cannot read prices / C. Farm names are too long
   - **答案：A** — 店主不想使用含糊的可持續發展說法。
3. What does the designer propose instead?
   - A. Traceable, specific production facts / B. A green symbol alone / C. Removing all labels
   - **答案：A** — 設計師建議列明農場、工場和運輸距離。

---

## b2-08 · Reviving an old biscuit

**B2 / 本地產品**

- **Nadia:** The bakery wants to bring back its oat biscuit from the 1950s, but the original recipe is extremely sweet.
- **Oliver:** If we reduce the sugar, can we honestly call it the same product?
- **Nadia:** Perhaps call it “inspired by” the original. We can keep the toasted-oat flavour and use less packaging.
- **Oliver:** That respects the history without claiming perfect authenticity. Let’s invite older customers to taste the trial batch.

1. What problem is found in the original recipe?
   - A. It contains too much sugar / B. It contains no oats / C. It cannot be baked
   - **答案：A** — 原食譜甜度極高。
2. Why use the phrase “inspired by”?
   - A. The recipe will be adapted / B. The bakery opened in the 1950s / C. Customers dislike history
   - **答案：A** — 產品保留特色但會減糖，不能聲稱完全相同。
3. Who will be asked to taste the trial?
   - A. Older customers / B. Only bakery staff / C. Packaging designers
   - **答案：A** — Oliver 建議邀請年長顧客試食。

---

## b2-09 · The red bicycle

**B2 / 童年回憶**

- **Saira:** My clearest early memory is riding a red bicycle in the courtyard, although my mother says the bicycle was blue.
- **Jon:** Perhaps you remember a photograph rather than the event itself.
- **Saira:** There is a photo, but it is black and white. I may have borrowed the red from my cousin’s bicycle years later.
- **Jon:** Memory often rebuilds a story from different pieces. That doesn’t make the feeling of independence unreal.

1. What detail is disputed?
   - A. The bicycle’s colour / B. The location / C. Saira’s ability to ride
   - **答案：A** — Saira 記得紅色，母親卻說是藍色。
2. Why can the photograph not confirm the colour?
   - A. It is black and white / B. It shows another child / C. It was taken years later
   - **答案：A** — 黑白相片不能證實顏色。
3. What is Jon’s view of memory?
   - A. It can combine details while preserving genuine feelings / B. It records every fact accurately / C. It is useless without photographs
   - **答案：A** — 他認為記憶會重組片段，但獨立感仍可真實。

---

## b2-10 · Learning to swim

**B2 / 童年回憶**

- **Patrick:** I used to say I learned to swim during a seaside holiday, but my sister insists it happened at the council pool.
- **Elena:** Do you remember the water or what people told you later?
- **Patrick:** Mostly the smell of chlorine and my father holding out a striped towel. That supports my sister, doesn’t it?
- **Elena:** Probably, although seaside pools also use chlorine. The towel may be a stronger clue if it appears in family photos from that period.

1. Where does Patrick’s sister say he learned?
   - A. At the council pool / B. In the sea / C. At school
   - **答案：A** — 姐姐堅持事情發生在市營泳池。
2. Which memory seems to support her account?
   - A. The smell of chlorine / B. The seaside weather / C. A boat trip
   - **答案：A** — 氯氣味較符合泳池情境，但並非絕對證據。
3. Why is Elena cautious?
   - A. Seaside pools can also smell of chlorine / B. Patrick has no sister / C. Striped towels did not exist
   - **答案：A** — Elena 指海邊泳池亦使用氯，因此不能單憑氣味確定。

---

## b2-11 · Recycling confusion

**B2 / 污染與回收**

- **Councillor:** Our survey shows residents want to recycle, yet a quarter of collected material is contaminated.
- **Resident:** The labels differ between flats, street bins and the council website. Motivation isn’t the only issue.
- **Councillor:** We’re designing one set of pictures for every location and adding feedback tags to rejected bins.
- **Resident:** Good. But tags should explain the mistake politely; otherwise people may feel blamed and give up.

1. What proportion of recycling is contaminated?
   - A. One quarter / B. One half / C. Three quarters
   - **答案：A** — 調查顯示四分之一收集物受到污染。
2. What cause does the resident identify?
   - A. Inconsistent instructions / B. A lack of street bins / C. No interest in recycling
   - **答案：A** — 不同地點和網站的標籤不一致，造成混亂。
3. Why should rejection tags be polite?
   - A. Blame may discourage participation / B. Polite tags cost less / C. Only children read them
   - **答案：A** — 責備語氣可能令人放棄回收。

---

## b2-12 · Traffic pollution near school

**B2 / 污染與回收**

- **Headteacher:** Air readings are highest during the morning drop-off, so we propose closing the road for forty minutes.
- **Parent:** That may shift cars to the next street rather than reduce them. Some parents drive because they continue to work outside town.
- **Headteacher:** We could create a drop-off point near the ring road and run a supervised walking group.
- **Parent:** I’d support a trial if the school measures surrounding streets as well as the gate.

1. When are pollution readings highest?
   - A. During morning drop-off / B. At lunchtime / C. After evening clubs
   - **答案：A** — 校門早上接送時段錄得最高污染。
2. What unintended effect worries the parent?
   - A. Traffic moving to a nearby street / B. Children walking too quickly / C. The school closing permanently
   - **答案：A** — 封路可能只把車流轉移到下一條街。
3. Under what condition will the parent support a trial?
   - A. Monitor nearby streets too / B. Ban all working parents / C. Build a new school gate
   - **答案：A** — 家長要求同時監測周邊街道。

---

## b2-13 · Remote work and community

**B2 / 主題討論**

- **Interviewer:** You argue that remote work can strengthen small towns. Isn’t isolation a serious drawback?
- **Beth:** It can be. My point is not that everyone should stay home, but that fewer long commutes leave time for local activities.
- **Interviewer:** Only if workers use that time locally.
- **Beth:** Exactly. That’s why shared workspaces matter: they offer social contact and bring customers to nearby cafés without requiring a journey to the city.

1. What claim is Beth actually making?
   - A. Reduced commuting can free time for local life / B. Everyone should work alone at home / C. Cities should ban offices
   - **答案：A** — 她並非主張人人留家，而是減少通勤可騰出社區時間。
2. What limitation does the interviewer raise?
   - A. Saved time may not benefit the town / B. Shared offices are illegal / C. Cafés dislike workers
   - **答案：A** — 只有當人們在本地使用所省時間，社區才受惠。
3. Why does Beth favour shared workspaces?
   - A. They combine social contact with local spending / B. They make commutes longer / C. They remove the need for cafés
   - **答案：A** — 共享工作間既減少孤立，也為附近商戶帶來顧客。

---

## b2-14 · Should museums return objects?

**B2 / 主題討論**

- **Tutor:** Your topic is the return of museum objects. Do you think every object should go back to its country of origin?
- **Malik:** A single rule would ignore how objects were acquired and what present communities want. Each case needs transparent evidence.
- **Tutor:** Could long-term loans be a compromise?
- **Malik:** Sometimes, but calling a loan a solution can preserve unequal ownership. Museums should first speak with the communities concerned, not decide the compromise alone.

1. Why does Malik reject one universal rule?
   - A. Cases have different histories and community wishes / B. Museums have no records / C. All objects were gifts
   - **答案：A** — 物件取得方式和當代社群意願各異。
2. What concern does he have about long-term loans?
   - A. They may leave unequal ownership unchanged / B. They are always too short / C. They prevent public display
   - **答案：A** — 借展可能看似解決，實際仍維持不平等擁有權。
3. Who should be consulted first?
   - A. The communities concerned / B. Only museum directors / C. Tourists at the entrance
   - **答案：A** — 他強調先與相關社群對話。

---

## b2-15 · Responding to a challenge

**B2 / 互動階段理解**

- **Examiner:** You say public transport should be free. Wouldn’t that be unfair to people who rarely use it?
- **Lina:** I understand the objection, but roads are also funded by people who do not drive. The wider benefit is less congestion and cleaner air.
- **Examiner:** Are those benefits guaranteed?
- **Lina:** No. Free fares must be combined with frequent, reliable services; otherwise existing passengers benefit but few drivers change their habits.

1. What objection does the examiner raise?
   - A. Non-users may subsidise the service / B. Buses are already too frequent / C. Drivers want cleaner air
   - **答案：A** — 提問質疑少用公共交通者亦需承擔費用是否公平。
2. How does Lina respond initially?
   - A. She compares it with shared road funding / B. She says benefits are guaranteed / C. She withdraws the proposal
   - **答案：A** — 她以非駕駛者也資助道路作類比。
3. What condition is necessary to change drivers’ habits?
   - A. Frequent and reliable service / B. Free fares alone / C. Higher parking availability
   - **答案：A** — Lina 說免費車資必須配合頻密可靠班次。

---

## b2-16 · Interpreting a hypothetical

**B2 / 互動階段理解**

- **Examiner:** If your local market closed, how might the area change?
- **Theo:** At first, supermarkets would gain customers, but the social effect could be greater than the economic one.
- **Examiner:** In what sense?
- **Theo:** The market is where new residents meet long-term neighbours. Without it, the area would lose an informal meeting place, even if another shop sold the same food.

1. What does “in what sense?” ask Theo to do?
   - A. Explain the social effect / B. List supermarket prices / C. Predict the closing date
   - **答案：A** — 考官要求他具體解釋「社會影響較大」的意思。
2. Who currently meet at the market?
   - A. New and long-term residents / B. Only supermarket staff / C. Tourists and farmers only
   - **答案：A** — 市場讓新居民與長期街坊相遇。
3. Why can another food shop not fully replace it?
   - A. The market also serves as a meeting place / B. The food is unavailable elsewhere / C. The market has free parking
   - **答案：A** — 相同商品不能取代其非正式社交功能。

---

## b2-17 · Delayed conference journey

**B2 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Manager:** The 8:40 train is cancelled. The next direct service arrives after the first conference session begins.
- **Delegate:** Could I take the 9:05 to Reading and change there?
- **Manager:** Yes, but the connection is only six minutes. If you miss it, you’ll arrive even later than on the direct train.
- **Delegate:** Then I’ll take the direct service and join the opening session online from the train. Please tell the organiser I’ll be there for the workshop.

1. What risk comes with changing at Reading?
   - A. The connection is very short / B. The ticket becomes invalid / C. The conference is cancelled
   - **答案：A** — 轉車只有六分鐘，錯過便更遲。
2. Which journey does the delegate choose?
   - A. The next direct service / B. The 9:05 with a change / C. A taxi to Reading
   - **答案：A** — 他決定乘下一班直達車。
3. How will the delegate handle the opening session?
   - A. Join online from the train / B. Skip the whole conference / C. Ask to move the workshop
   - **答案：A** — 他會在列車上網上參與開場環節。

---

## b2-18 · Community grant decision

**B2 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Chair:** We can fund either the youth theatre’s equipment or the garden group’s accessible paths, not both in full.
- **Committee member:** The theatre could borrow lights from the college, whereas the paths cannot be created without materials.
- **Chair:** So give the garden the full amount and reject the theatre?
- **Committee member:** Not necessarily. Fund the paths, then offer the theatre a smaller grant for transport to the college. That supports both aims, though not both original requests.

1. Why is the garden project harder to do without funding?
   - A. It needs physical materials / B. It cannot borrow actors / C. It has no committee
   - **答案：A** — 無材料便不能興建無障礙通道。
2. What does the committee member propose for the theatre?
   - A. A smaller transport grant / B. The full equipment budget / C. No support at all
   - **答案：A** — 建議給較小款項，用於前往學院借燈的交通。
3. What principle guides the proposal?
   - A. Support both aims by adapting the requests / B. Always split money equally / C. Reject projects that borrow equipment
   - **答案：A** — 方案調整用途，使兩個目標均獲支援。

---

## b2-19 · Radio interview correction

**B2 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Host:** Your study proves that listening to music improves everyone’s concentration.
- **Dr Reed:** I need to correct that. We found a small improvement among adults doing familiar tasks, not among every participant or every kind of work.
- **Host:** So should offices play music?
- **Dr Reed:** The study cannot answer that. Participants chose their own music and used headphones; a shared office playlist would be a different condition.

1. What does Dr Reed correct?
   - A. The host has generalised the findings too far / B. The study involved no music / C. All participants were children
   - **答案：A** — 研究只在特定成人及熟悉任務中發現小幅改善，不能推廣至所有人。
2. Which condition was used in the study?
   - A. Participants chose music and wore headphones / B. Offices used one shared playlist / C. Participants performed only new tasks
   - **答案：A** — 參與者自行選歌並用耳機。
3. Why can the study not justify office playlists?
   - A. A shared playlist is a different condition / B. Music never affects concentration / C. Offices have no headphones
   - **答案：A** — 集體播放與實驗條件不同，不能直接推論。

---

## b2-20 · Repair café instructions

**B2 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Organiser:** Before opening, test every donated appliance for electrical safety. A green label means it passed last month, but check the date rather than assuming it is current.
- **Volunteer:** What if an item fails but the fault looks simple?
- **Organiser:** Move it to the red shelf and record the fault. Do not repair electrical items until Kamal, our qualified technician, arrives.
- **Volunteer:** Understood. I can examine torn clothing meanwhile because that station does not require him.

1. Why must volunteers check green-label dates?
   - A. A previous test may no longer be current / B. Green labels indicate failure / C. Labels show repair prices
   - **答案：A** — 綠標只代表上月曾通過，仍需核對日期。
2. What happens to a failed appliance first?
   - A. It goes on the red shelf and is recorded / B. It is repaired immediately / C. It is returned without a note
   - **答案：A** — 失敗物品先移至紅架並記錄故障。
3. Which work can the volunteer do before Kamal arrives?
   - A. Examine torn clothing / B. Repair electrical appliances / C. Approve safety tests
   - **答案：A** — 衣物站不需要合資格電器技師。

---

## b1-21 · A festival stall

**B1 / 節日**

- **Erin:** The spring festival needs volunteers on Sunday. Would you rather sell plants or welcome visitors?
- **Josh:** I know very little about plants, so welcoming people would suit me better.
- **Erin:** The welcome desk opens at ten, but volunteers should meet at nine thirty for instructions.
- **Josh:** I can do that. I will bring a water bottle because we will be outside.

1. Which job does Josh prefer?
   - A. Selling plants / B. Giving instructions / C. Welcoming visitors
   - **答案：C** — Josh 說自己較適合迎接訪客。
2. When should volunteers meet?
   - A. At 10:30 / B. At 9:30 / C. At 10:00
   - **答案：B** — 義工須於九時半集合聽取指示。
3. Why will Josh bring water?
   - A. He will work outdoors / B. The desk sells drinks / C. He is watering plants
   - **答案：A** — 他會在戶外工作，所以帶水。

---

## b1-22 · A missed bus

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Sara:** The number six has just left. The next one is in twenty minutes.
- **Milo:** Our appointment starts in half an hour. Could we walk to the clinic instead?
- **Sara:** Yes, it takes fifteen minutes, and the route through the park is shorter than the main road.
- **Milo:** Let’s walk through the park then. I will ring the clinic if we are delayed.

1. How long is the wait for the next bus?
   - A. Half an hour / B. Twenty minutes / C. Fifteen minutes
   - **答案：B** — 下一班巴士要等二十分鐘。
2. Which route do they choose?
   - A. Through the park / B. Along the main road / C. By the number six bus
   - **答案：A** — 他們決定步行穿過公園。
3. When will Milo ring the clinic?
   - A. Before leaving the bus stop / B. After the appointment / C. If they are delayed
   - **答案：C** — Milo 說若延誤便會致電診所。

---

## b1-23 · A leaving card

**B1 / 特別場合**

- **Nora:** It is Peter’s last day on Thursday. Shall we get him a leaving card?
- **Alex:** Yes. I can buy one today, but I do not know everyone on his team.
- **Nora:** I will take it round the office tomorrow so people can write a message.
- **Alex:** Good. Let’s give it to him after lunch, before he leaves for his train.

1. What will Alex buy?
   - A. A card / B. A train ticket / C. Lunch
   - **答案：A** — Alex 主動買一張卡。
2. When will Nora take it round?
   - A. Next week / B. After Peter leaves / C. Tomorrow
   - **答案：C** — Nora 會在明天把卡拿給同事簽寫。
3. When will Peter receive it?
   - A. On the train / B. After lunch / C. Before breakfast
   - **答案：B** — 他們打算午飯後、Peter 趕火車前送卡。

---

## b1-24 · A play at the hall

**B1 / 娛樂**

- **Chloe:** The village play is on Friday and Saturday. Which evening are you free?
- **Arun:** Friday, although I cannot arrive before seven because of work.
- **Chloe:** The doors open at seven and the play starts at seven thirty. Shall I reserve two seats?
- **Arun:** Please do. I will pay you back when we meet at the hall.

1. Which evening suits Arun?
   - A. Saturday / B. Neither evening / C. Friday
   - **答案：C** — Arun 星期五有空。
2. When does the play begin?
   - A. Before 7:00 / B. At 7:30 / C. At 7:00
   - **答案：B** — 七時開門，七時半演出開始。
3. What will Chloe reserve?
   - A. Two seats / B. A parking space / C. A table for dinner
   - **答案：A** — Chloe 提議預留兩個座位。

---

## b1-25 · A music playlist

**B1 / 音樂**

- **Finn:** I am making a playlist for the school fair. Should every song be fast?
- **Lara:** A mixture would be better. Some people may want to sit and talk.
- **Finn:** Good point. I will ask the teachers to check the words before we play anything.
- **Lara:** I can suggest some quieter songs tonight, if that helps.

1. What does Lara recommend?
   - A. No music at the fair / B. A mixture of song speeds / C. Only fast songs
   - **答案：B** — Lara 建議快慢歌曲混合。
2. Who will check the words?
   - A. The teachers / B. The visitors / C. The musicians
   - **答案：A** — Finn 會請老師檢查歌詞。
3. What will Lara suggest?
   - A. A different venue / B. More teachers / C. Quieter songs
   - **答案：C** — Lara 會提供較安靜歌曲的建議。

---

## b1-26 · A first pottery class

**B1 / 近期個人經歷**

- **Mina:** How did your pottery class go?
- **Jack:** I made a bowl, but one side was thicker than the other.
- **Mina:** Will you take it home today?
- **Jack:** Not yet. It must dry and go in the kiln first. The teacher will email us when it is ready.

1. What did Jack make?
   - A. A bowl / B. A plate / C. A cup
   - **答案：A** — Jack 製作了一個碗。
2. What was uneven?
   - A. The colour of the clay / B. The temperature of the kiln / C. The thickness of the sides
   - **答案：C** — 碗的一邊較厚。
3. How will Jack know it is ready?
   - A. Mina will collect it / B. The teacher will email him / C. He must phone every day
   - **答案：B** — 老師會在作品準備好時發電郵。

---

## b1-27 · A favourite walk

**B1 / 自選主題討論**

- **Tutor:** Tell me about a place you enjoy walking.
- **Evie:** I like the canal path near my flat. It is peaceful even though the road is close.
- **Tutor:** Do you always walk alone?
- **Evie:** Usually, but on Saturdays my neighbour joins me and we stop for tea afterwards.

1. Where does Evie enjoy walking?
   - A. Beside the beach / B. In a shopping centre / C. Along the canal
   - **答案：C** — Evie 喜歡在寓所附近的運河小徑散步。
2. How does she describe the path?
   - A. Difficult to find / B. Peaceful / C. Crowded and noisy
   - **答案：B** — 她形容小徑寧靜。
3. Who joins her on Saturdays?
   - A. Her neighbour / B. Her tutor / C. Her brother
   - **答案：A** — 星期六，她的鄰居會同行。

---

## b1-28 · Asking for an example

**B1 / 理解提問**

- **Examiner:** What makes a good celebration?
- **Omar:** For me, it is when everyone feels included, even if the event is small.
- **Examiner:** Could you give me an example of that?
- **Omar:** At my cousin’s party, we chose games that both children and grandparents could enjoy.

1. Which response best answers the examiner’s follow-up?
   - A. Could we change the subject? / B. At my cousin’s party, we chose games for all ages. / C. The party is next Tuesday.
   - **答案：B** — 考官要求舉例；描述適合各年齡人士的遊戲直接回應問題。
2. What matters most to Omar?
   - A. Including everyone / B. Inviting a large crowd / C. Buying expensive decorations
   - **答案：A** — 他認為每個人都能參與最重要。
3. Who could enjoy the games?
   - A. Only children / B. Only the hosts / C. Both children and grandparents
   - **答案：C** — 遊戲適合小孩和祖父母一起玩。

---

## b1-29 · A parcel collection

**B1 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Assistant:** Your parcel is ready to collect from the shop until Friday evening.
- **Customer:** I am working late. Can my sister collect it for me?
- **Assistant:** Yes, if she brings the collection code and her own identification.
- **Customer:** That is helpful. I will send her the code now.

1. When is the collection deadline?
   - A. Friday evening / B. Thursday morning / C. Saturday evening
   - **答案：A** — 包裹最遲可於星期五晚上領取。
2. What must the sister bring?
   - A. The customer’s bank card / B. A printed receipt only / C. The code and her identification
   - **答案：C** — 代領者須帶領取碼及自己的身份證明。
3. What will the customer do now?
   - A. Cancel the parcel / B. Send the code to their sister / C. Visit the shop
   - **答案：B** — 顧客會立即將領取碼傳給代領的家人。

---

## b1-30 · A shared celebration

**B1 / 節日**

- **Beth:** For the neighbourhood celebration, shall we put the tables in the garden?
- **Yusuf:** Only if it stays dry. The hall is available as a backup.
- **Beth:** I will check the forecast on Friday and tell the guests where to go.
- **Yusuf:** I can put signs at both entrances so late arrivals can find us.

1. What is the backup venue?
   - A. The library / B. A café / C. The hall
   - **答案：C** — 如天氣欠佳，會堂可作後備場地。
2. When will Beth check the forecast?
   - A. Next month / B. On Friday / C. On the morning after the event
   - **答案：B** — Beth 會於星期五查看天氣預報。
3. Why will Yusuf put up signs?
   - A. To help late arrivals / B. To advertise a café / C. To reserve the garden
   - **答案：A** — 標示可幫助遲到的人找到場地。

---

## b1-31 · Booking a ferry

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Paula:** The ferry website offers a morning crossing and an afternoon crossing.
- **Reece:** The afternoon one is cheaper, but we would miss the guided walk on the island.
- **Paula:** Then the morning ticket is worth the extra cost. It includes a bicycle space too.
- **Reece:** Fine. I will book it after checking that the walk still has places.

1. Why favour the morning crossing?
   - A. To avoid taking bicycles / B. To attend the guided walk / C. Because it is cheaper
   - **答案：B** — 下午渡輪雖較便宜，卻趕不上導賞步行。
2. What is included in the morning ticket?
   - A. A bicycle space / B. A guided lunch / C. A hotel room
   - **答案：A** — 早上船票包括單車位置。
3. What will Reece check before booking?
   - A. The hotel menu / B. The ferry captain’s name / C. Places on the walk
   - **答案：C** — Reece 先確認導賞步行是否仍有名額。

---

## b1-32 · A new recipe

**B1 / 近期個人經歷**

- **Tara:** I made the soup you recommended, but I used carrots instead of squash.
- **Elliot:** Did it work?
- **Tara:** Yes, although I added too much pepper. A spoonful of yoghurt made it milder.
- **Elliot:** I will remember that. I am making it for my family this weekend.

1. What did Tara use instead of squash?
   - A. Carrots / B. Potatoes / C. Yoghurt
   - **答案：A** — Tara 用紅蘿蔔代替南瓜。
2. What made the soup milder?
   - A. Extra pepper / B. More squash / C. Yoghurt
   - **答案：C** — 加入乳酪令湯沒那麼辣。
3. When will Elliot make the soup?
   - A. Next month / B. This weekend / C. Yesterday
   - **答案：B** — Elliot 打算本週末煮給家人吃。

---

## b1-33 · A change at the library

**B1 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Librarian:** The reading group normally meets upstairs, but the lift is being repaired today.
- **Visitor:** Will the meeting be cancelled?
- **Librarian:** No, it will be in the ground-floor room at two, as planned. Please use the garden entrance.
- **Visitor:** Thank you. I will tell my friend, who is bringing the books.

1. Why has the room changed?
   - A. The books have not arrived / B. The garden is closed / C. The lift is being repaired
   - **答案：C** — 升降機維修，因此更換房間。
2. What remains unchanged?
   - A. The room / B. The meeting time / C. The entrance
   - **答案：B** — 聚會仍在原定兩時舉行。
3. Which entrance should visitors use?
   - A. The garden entrance / B. The upstairs entrance / C. The car park entrance
   - **答案：A** — 館員指示使用花園入口。

---

## b2-21 · A practical assessment

**B2 / 教育**

- **Lecturer:** The department is considering replacing the final written exam with a group project.
- **Student:** That could test collaboration, but individual contributions might be hard to distinguish.
- **Lecturer:** We could combine the project mark with a short individual reflection explaining each person’s decisions.
- **Student:** That sounds fairer, provided the marking criteria are published before the project begins.

1. What concern does the student raise?
   - A. Published criteria would prevent collaboration / B. Individual work may be difficult to identify / C. A reflection would replace the group project
   - **答案：B** — 學生擔心難以分辨個人貢獻。
2. What would supplement the group mark?
   - A. An individual reflection / B. A separate written exam / C. An assessment by other groups
   - **答案：A** — 建議以個人反思補充小組成績。
3. What condition does the student attach?
   - A. Let each group set its own criteria / B. Publish criteria after the project / C. Publish criteria in advance
   - **答案：C** — 學生要求事先公布評分準則。

---

## b2-22 · Welcoming guests

**B2 / 國家習俗**

- **Amina:** At my grandmother’s house, guests are offered tea before anyone discusses business.
- **Rob:** In my family we tend to get straight to the reason for the visit. Neither approach is necessarily unfriendly.
- **Amina:** Exactly. Problems arise when people assume their own custom is the only polite one.
- **Rob:** Perhaps a host can explain what to expect, especially when guests are unfamiliar with the routine.

1. What happens first at Amina’s grandmother’s house?
   - A. Guests are offered tea / B. Business is discussed / C. Guests make their own meal
   - **答案：A** — 客人到訪時先獲奉茶。
2. What do the speakers agree?
   - A. Direct discussion is always rude / B. Guests should never ask questions / C. Different customs can both be polite
   - **答案：C** — 兩人同意不同習慣都可以有禮貌。
3. What does Rob suggest hosts do?
   - A. Replace family customs with formal rules / B. Explain unfamiliar routines / C. Expect guests to discover the routine
   - **答案：B** — Rob 建議向不熟悉習慣的客人解釋安排。

---

## b2-23 · A village health service

**B2 / 鄉村與城市生活**

- **Councillor:** The mobile clinic visits the village once a fortnight, but some appointments go unused.
- **Nurse:** Residents told us the booking line is open only during working hours, when many cannot call.
- **Councillor:** We could extend the phone hours and add an online form.
- **Nurse:** Both would help, but please keep a telephone option for people who do not use the internet.

1. What might explain the unused appointments?
   - A. The village has no demand for appointments / B. Online booking has already failed / C. Some residents face barriers to booking
   - **答案：C** — 部分居民在預約電話開放時未能致電，因此有空位不代表沒有人需要服務。
2. What booking problem did residents identify?
   - A. The clinic accepts only online bookings / B. The phone line opens only during working hours / C. Appointments can only be booked in person
   - **答案：B** — 電話預約只於工作時間開放，部分居民不方便致電。
3. Why retain telephone booking?
   - A. Some people do not use the internet / B. Longer phone hours have been rejected / C. Online forms cannot handle appointments
   - **答案：A** — 護士提醒有些人不上網，應保留電話選項。

---

## b2-24 · A local cheese label

**B2 / 本地產品**

- **Producer:** Our cheese is made here, but the milk comes from farms across the county.
- **Retailer:** Then a label saying “made in the village” is accurate, though customers might assume the milk comes from the village too.
- **Producer:** We could name the dairy and explain where the milk comes from on the same label.
- **Retailer:** That gives shoppers useful detail without suggesting every ingredient comes from one place.

1. Where does the milk come from?
   - A. Another country / B. Farms across the county / C. Only one village farm
   - **答案：B** — 牛奶來自郡內各農場。
2. Why does the retailer favour a more detailed label?
   - A. An accurate statement can still give a misleading impression / B. County-wide sourcing makes the village claim false / C. Customers should choose only cheese made from village milk
   - **答案：A** — 「村內製造」雖然屬實，仍可能令人誤以為牛奶亦來自村內；補充來源可避免誤解。
3. What information will clarify the label?
   - A. The farm name without the dairy / B. The dairy name without the milk’s origin / C. The dairy and the milk’s origin
   - **答案：C** — 標籤會說明乳品廠及牛奶來源。

---

## b2-25 · A remembered classroom

**B2 / 童年回憶**

- **Molly:** I remember our primary classroom as enormous, but I visited recently and it seemed quite small.
- **Hassan:** You were much smaller then. Your memory of the space may be accurate from a child’s perspective.
- **Molly:** Possibly. The windows have changed too, so I cannot assume everything is exactly as it was.
- **Hassan:** A photograph from that time might help, although it would show only one angle.

1. What surprised Molly on her visit?
   - A. The classroom seemed smaller / B. The windows were unchanged / C. The classroom seemed larger
   - **答案：A** — 重訪時課室比她記憶中小。
2. How does Hassan explain her memory?
   - A. The room must have been rebuilt / B. The changed windows explain everything / C. A child experiences scale differently
   - **答案：C** — Hassan 認為孩童體型較小，感受空間大小的角度不同。
3. What limit does Hassan mention about a photograph?
   - A. It could confirm the room’s exact dimensions / B. It shows only one angle / C. It would show only the new windows
   - **答案：B** — 一張相片只顯示單一角度。

---

## b2-26 · Reducing food waste

**B2 / 污染與回收**

- **Manager:** We throw away more prepared sandwiches on Mondays than on any other day.
- **Cook:** Monday sales vary because nearby offices have different attendance patterns each week.
- **Manager:** Instead of a fixed quantity, let us prepare a smaller first batch and make more if demand rises.
- **Cook:** That could reduce waste, provided we monitor whether customers have to wait too long.

1. Why is a fixed Monday quantity problematic?
   - A. Monday demand is always higher than expected / B. Office workers no longer buy sandwiches / C. Office attendance makes demand vary
   - **答案：C** — 附近辦公室每星期的出勤情況不同，令星期一銷量不穩定，固定製作數量容易造成浪費。
2. What production change is proposed?
   - A. Prepare all Monday sandwiches on Sunday / B. Make a smaller batch first / C. Prepare the same larger batch every Monday
   - **答案：B** — 建議先做較少數量，有需求再增加。
3. What should be monitored during the change?
   - A. Customer waiting time / B. Office attendance on Fridays / C. Demand for a wider menu
   - **答案：A** — 廚師提醒須留意顧客會否等太久。

---

## b2-27 · Sharing public space

**B2 / 主題討論**

- **Tutor:** You propose more seating in the town square. How would you answer people who fear it will obstruct events?
- **Grace:** The seats need not be permanent. Movable benches could be rearranged when a market or performance takes place.
- **Tutor:** Would movable furniture create any difficulties?
- **Grace:** Storage and maintenance would cost money, so the council should test a few benches before buying a full set.

1. How does Grace address the concern about events?
   - A. Move every event to another venue / B. Use movable benches / C. Keep permanent benches around each stall
   - **答案：B** — 可移動長椅能在活動時重新安排。
2. What drawback does Grace acknowledge?
   - A. Storage and maintenance cost money / B. Movable benches require permanent foundations / C. Events would have to stop during the trial
   - **答案：A** — 她承認儲存及維修需要費用。
3. What does her proposed trial suggest?
   - A. She has abandoned the seating proposal / B. She believes a full set would cost nothing / C. She wants to assess practical costs before committing
   - **答案：C** — 她建議先試用少量長椅，反映她希望了解實際儲存及維修需要，再決定是否全面購置。

---

## b2-28 · Qualifying an opinion

**B2 / 互動階段理解**

- **Examiner:** You said technology makes people less sociable. Do you think that is always true?
- **Dev:** No, I should qualify that. Video calls help my family stay in touch across countries.
- **Examiner:** Then what concerns you?
- **Dev:** When people check their phones throughout a meal, those present may feel ignored. The effect depends on how technology is used.

1. How does Dev revise his original claim?
   - A. It is not always true / B. It applies to video calls in particular / C. It is true whenever families live apart
   - **答案：A** — Dev 收窄原先的說法，承認並非總是如此。
2. What benefit does he mention?
   - A. Phones improve conversation at every meal / B. Technology prevents families from moving apart / C. Video calls connect distant family
   - **答案：C** — 視像通話幫助身處不同國家的家人保持聯繫。
3. What determines the effect in Dev’s view?
   - A. How far apart people live / B. How technology is used / C. Whether contact happens face to face
   - **答案：B** — 他認為影響取決於使用方式。

---

## b2-29 · A workshop waiting list

**B2 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Organiser:** The Saturday repair workshop is full, but we have a waiting list in case anyone cancels.
- **Attendee:** I can only attend in the morning. Would being on the list commit me to the whole day?
- **Organiser:** No. We will contact you if a place opens, and you can decide then. The morning session covers bicycles.
- **Attendee:** Please add me. If no place becomes available, I will use the online guide instead.

1. Why is there a waiting list?
   - A. The online guide is unavailable / B. The morning session is cancelled / C. The workshop is full
   - **答案：C** — 工作坊已滿，候補名單用於有人取消時。
2. What does joining the waiting list involve?
   - A. A guaranteed place in the morning session / B. An opportunity to accept a place later / C. A commitment to attend the whole day
   - **答案：B** — 候補名單只提供日後接受空位的機會，並不保證有位，也不要求參加全日。
3. What is the attendee’s alternative?
   - A. Use the online guide / B. Attend on Sunday / C. Book the afternoon session
   - **答案：A** — 若沒有空位，參加者會使用網上指南。

---

## b2-30 · Feedback after a presentation

**B2 / 教育**

- **Tutor:** Your presentation was well researched, but the audience struggled to follow the final section.
- **Student:** I added several statistics at the last minute. Was the slide too crowded?
- **Tutor:** Partly. More importantly, you did not explain how the figures supported your conclusion.
- **Student:** I will select fewer figures and make that connection explicit when I revise it.

1. What did the audience struggle with?
   - A. The research topic itself / B. The final section / C. The introduction only
   - **答案：B** — 聽眾難以跟上最後部分。
2. What was the main issue with the figures?
   - A. Their link to the conclusion was unexplained / B. They were all incorrect / C. There were no figures
   - **答案：A** — 導師最關注數據與結論的關係沒有解釋。
3. How will the student revise?
   - A. Add every available statistic / B. Remove the conclusion / C. Use fewer figures and explain their relevance
   - **答案：C** — 學生會精選數據並說明其關聯。

---

## b2-31 · A gift for a host

**B2 / 國家習俗**

- **Lena:** Your aunt has invited me to dinner and I thought I would bring flowers. Is that appropriate?
- **Harish:** Usually, but my aunt is allergic to strong scents. Something edible might be easier.
- **Lena:** I could bring biscuits, though I should ask whether anyone has dietary restrictions.
- **Harish:** That is considerate. Asking beforehand is more useful than guessing what a host expects.

1. Why might flowers be unsuitable?
   - A. The host has a scent allergy / B. The host has a dietary restriction / C. The host expects guests to bring biscuits
   - **答案：A** — 女主人對濃烈香味敏感。
2. What will Lena check about biscuits?
   - A. Whether every guest prefers sweet gifts / B. Whether the host expects expensive brands / C. Dietary restrictions
   - **答案：C** — Lena 會先詢問有沒有飲食限制。
3. What principle does Harish favour?
   - A. Assume edible gifts are always suitable / B. Ask instead of guessing / C. Bring the same gift to every host
   - **答案：B** — Harish 認為預先詢問比猜測更周到。

---

## b2-32 · A refill scheme

**B2 / 污染與回收**

- **Owner:** We are introducing refill containers for cleaning liquid. Customers pay a deposit for the bottle.
- **Customer:** Does that mean I must return it to this branch?
- **Owner:** Any of our branches will accept it. The deposit is returned even if you decide not to refill.
- **Customer:** That makes the scheme more flexible. Clear signs would help people understand the deposit is refundable.

1. Where can the bottle be returned?
   - A. Only at this branch / B. At any supermarket / C. At any branch
   - **答案：C** — 任何一間該店分店都接受退瓶。
2. What does the owner clarify about the deposit?
   - A. It can only be used as credit for another refill / B. A refill purchase is not required for a refund / C. It can be refunded only at the original branch
   - **答案：B** — 退瓶時即使不再補充清潔液，按金仍會退還。
3. Why does the customer want clear signs?
   - A. To explain that the deposit is refundable / B. To explain why returns are branch-specific / C. To encourage customers to buy another bottle
   - **答案：A** — 清晰標示可讓顧客知道按金可退。

---

## b2-33 · A survey result

**B2 / 綜合聆聽**

- **Researcher:** Our survey found that sixty per cent of respondents supported the proposed cycle lane.
- **Presenter:** Can I say sixty per cent of all residents support it?
- **Researcher:** Not safely. The survey was voluntary and may not represent residents who did not respond.
- **Presenter:** I will report the figure as a share of respondents and mention that limitation. We can gather further evidence before the decision.

1. Who made up the sixty per cent?
   - A. All cyclists in the country / B. Survey respondents / C. All residents
   - **答案：B** — 百分之六十的基數是調查回應者。
2. Why should the result not be generalised automatically?
   - A. Voluntary respondents may not represent everyone / B. Sixty per cent is too small a majority to report / C. Only surveys with unanimous answers are useful
   - **答案：A** — 自願回應者可能不能代表所有居民。
3. How will the presenter describe the figure?
   - A. As a proven share of all residents / B. As a share of all local cyclists / C. As a share of respondents
   - **答案：C** — 發表者會清楚指出比例來自回應者。

---

## b1-34 · Costumes for the parade

**B1 / 節日**

- **Hannah:** Have you chosen a costume for Saturday’s town parade? I am going as a sunflower.
- **Owen:** I want to dress as a tree, but I cannot find a brown shirt.
- **Hannah:** The theatre lends costumes for five pounds. You must return them by Monday evening.
- **Owen:** I will try there. Borrowing one would save me buying clothes I might only wear once.

1. What will Hannah dress as?
   - A. A sunflower / B. A tree / C. A butterfly
   - **答案：A** — Hannah 說自己會扮向日葵；想扮樹的是 Owen。
2. When must borrowed costumes be returned?
   - A. Before Saturday morning / B. By Sunday lunchtime / C. By Monday evening
   - **答案：C** — Hannah 說借來的服裝必須在星期一晚上前歸還。
3. Why does Owen like the idea of borrowing?
   - A. He wants to keep the costume permanently / B. He may only need the clothes once / C. He has no time to visit the theatre
   - **答案：B** — Owen 不想為可能只穿一次的服裝花錢，所以認為借用較合適。

---

## b1-35 · Festival baking shift

**B1 / 節日**

- **Priya:** The village food festival needs cakes for its charity stall. Can you bake any?
- **Tom:** I can make lemon cakes, although I cannot deliver them until Friday afternoon.
- **Priya:** That is fine. The stall opens on Saturday, and we have space in the school kitchen.
- **Tom:** Great. Please keep them away from the nut cakes because mine are nut-free.

1. What will Tom bake for the stall?
   - A. Nut cakes / B. Apple pies / C. Lemon cakes
   - **答案：C** — Tom 會做檸檬蛋糕。
2. When can Tom deliver his baking?
   - A. Thursday evening / B. Friday afternoon / C. Saturday morning
   - **答案：B** — 他說星期五下午才可以送到。
3. Why should the cakes be kept apart?
   - A. His cakes contain no nuts / B. His cakes need to stay hot / C. His cakes are for the school
   - **答案：A** — 他的蛋糕不含果仁，因此要與果仁蛋糕分開。

---

## b1-36 · A rainy market day

**B1 / 節日**

- **Megan:** The harvest festival market was moved indoors because of the rain.
- **Lewis:** I wondered why the square was empty. Is it in the sports hall?
- **Megan:** Yes, beside the swimming pool. The vegetable stalls are there until four.
- **Lewis:** Brilliant. I will go after lunch and buy some local honey too.

1. Why was the harvest market moved?
   - A. Too few sellers arrived / B. It was raining / C. The square was being repaired
   - **答案：B** — 因為下雨，市集改在室內舉行。
2. Where is the festival market now?
   - A. In the sports hall / B. In the swimming pool / C. In the town library
   - **答案：A** — 市集現時在體育館。
3. What else does Lewis hope to buy?
   - A. Fresh bread / B. Garden flowers / C. Local honey
   - **答案：C** — Lewis 還想買本地蜜糖。

---

## b1-37 · Festival wristbands

**B1 / 節日**

- **Aisha:** Have you collected your wristband for the town festival yet?
- **Ben:** Not yet. The email says I can collect it from the museum on Thursday.
- **Aisha:** That is right, but take your booking email and some identification.
- **Ben:** Thanks. I nearly went to the town hall instead.

1. What does Ben still need to collect?
   - A. His festival wristband / B. A museum guide / C. A bus ticket
   - **答案：A** — Ben 尚未領取城鎮節慶的入場手帶。
2. Which place is the collection point?
   - A. The town hall / B. The bus station / C. The museum
   - **答案：C** — 電郵說可在博物館領取。
3. What should Ben bring to collect it?
   - A. A letter from his employer / B. His booking email and identification / C. A printed festival poster
   - **答案：B** — 他需要帶預訂電郵和身分證明。

---

## b1-38 · Dancing at the fair

**B1 / 節日**

- **Ruth:** The community fair has a Scottish dancing lesson at two. Do you fancy trying it?
- **Calum:** I have never danced before, but beginners are welcome, according to the programme.
- **Ruth:** They are. Comfortable shoes are recommended, and the lesson is free.
- **Calum:** Then count me in. I will leave my heavy boots at home.

1. What activity interests Ruth?
   - A. A cooking lesson / B. A craft competition / C. A Scottish dancing lesson
   - **答案：C** — Ruth 提議參加蘇格蘭舞蹈課。
2. Who can join the dancing session?
   - A. Only children / B. Beginners can join / C. Only experienced dancers
   - **答案：B** — 節目表表示初學者也可參加。
3. What footwear will Calum avoid?
   - A. Heavy boots / B. Comfortable trainers / C. Soft dancing shoes
   - **答案：A** — Calum 說會把厚重的靴留在家中。

---

## b1-39 · The festival clean-up

**B1 / 節日**

- **Ellie:** Could you help clear the park after the autumn festival on Monday?
- **George:** Yes. Do we need to bring rubbish bags?
- **Ellie:** The council will provide those. Please bring gardening gloves, though.
- **George:** I have a pair. Shall we meet at the park gate at nine?

1. When will they clear the park?
   - A. On Saturday / B. On Monday / C. On Sunday
   - **答案：B** — 他們星期一會清理公園。
2. Who will provide the rubbish bags?
   - A. The council / B. The festival visitors / C. The school
   - **答案：A** — 垃圾袋由市議會提供。
3. What should George bring for the clean-up?
   - A. A garden chair / B. A box of bags / C. Gardening gloves
   - **答案：C** — 他需要帶園藝手套。

---

## b1-40 · A platform alteration

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Nina:** Our train to York is now leaving from platform five instead of platform two.
- **Daniel:** Good thing you checked the board. Does it still leave at twenty past ten?
- **Nina:** Yes, but the lift to platform five is broken, so we must use the stairs.
- **Daniel:** All right. I can carry your suitcase for you.

1. Which platform will the York train use?
   - A. Platform five / B. Platform two / C. Platform eight
   - **答案：A** — 往約克的列車改在五號月台開出。
2. What remains unchanged about the train?
   - A. Its platform number / B. Its arrival time / C. Its departure time
   - **答案：C** — Nina 確認列車仍然在十時二十分開出，開車時間沒有改動。
3. Why must they take the stairs?
   - A. The platform is closed / B. The lift is broken / C. The escalator is crowded
   - **答案：B** — 往五號月台的升降機壞了。

---

## b1-41 · The borrowed bicycle

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Amir:** I borrowed a bike from the station, but the front light does not work.
- **Beth:** You can exchange it at the cycle shop next to the entrance.
- **Amir:** Will I have to pay again?
- **Beth:** No, show them your hire receipt and they will replace the bike.

1. What is wrong with the hired bicycle?
   - A. Its back tyre is flat / B. Its bell is missing / C. Its front light does not work
   - **答案：C** — 借來的單車前燈不能運作。
2. Where can Amir exchange the bike?
   - A. At the repair centre in town / B. At the shop by the station entrance / C. At the bus office
   - **答案：B** — 他可以到車站入口旁的單車店更換。
3. What evidence of hire should Amir show?
   - A. The hire receipt / B. A bank statement / C. A driving licence
   - **答案：A** — Beth 說要出示租借收據。

---

## b1-42 · A bus ticket choice

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Fiona:** I need to take three buses today. Should I buy a return ticket?
- **Jack:** A day ticket might be cheaper if you are making several journeys.
- **Fiona:** Can I buy one from the driver with my bank card?
- **Jack:** Yes, and it is valid until the last bus tonight.

1. Which ticket does Jack suggest?
   - A. A return ticket / B. A day ticket / C. A weekly ticket
   - **答案：B** — Jack 建議買一日票。
2. How can Fiona pay the driver?
   - A. With a bank card / B. With a travel voucher only / C. With a cheque
   - **答案：A** — 她可以用銀行卡向司機付款。
3. How long can Fiona use the suggested ticket?
   - A. For twenty-four hours from purchase / B. Until midday tomorrow / C. Until the last bus tonight
   - **答案：C** — 一日票有效至今晚尾班車。

---

## b1-43 · Getting to the island

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Sophie:** The ferry to the island is cancelled this morning because the wind is too strong.
- **Ethan:** That is a shame. Is there another way across?
- **Sophie:** There is a bridge, but the bus over it does not leave until midday.
- **Ethan:** I will take that bus. I can read in the café while I wait.

1. Why is the morning ferry cancelled?
   - A. The wind is too strong / B. The boat needs cleaning / C. The tide is unusually low
   - **答案：A** — 由於風太大，早上的渡輪取消了。
2. How can Ethan reach the island instead?
   - A. By train through a tunnel / B. By walking along the beach / C. By bus over the bridge
   - **答案：C** — 他可以乘巴士經大橋前往。
3. What will Ethan do while waiting?
   - A. Buy a ferry ticket / B. Read in a café / C. Visit the harbour museum
   - **答案：B** — 他會在咖啡店看書等候。

---

## b1-44 · Meeting at the coach stop

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Lara:** The coach stop has moved while the road outside the cinema is repaired.
- **Reece:** Where should I wait for the airport coach now?
- **Lara:** Outside the post office, opposite the supermarket. It is only a five-minute walk.
- **Reece:** I will leave a little earlier so I have time to find it.

1. Why has the coach stop moved?
   - A. The cinema is being demolished / B. The supermarket is expanding / C. Road repairs are taking place
   - **答案：C** — 戲院外的道路正在維修，所以車站搬遷。
2. Where is the temporary coach stop?
   - A. Inside the supermarket / B. Outside the post office / C. Outside the cinema
   - **答案：B** — 臨時車站在郵局外面。
3. Why will Reece set off earlier?
   - A. To allow time to find the stop / B. To shop at the supermarket / C. To collect a parcel
   - **答案：A** — 他想預留時間尋找車站。

---

## b1-45 · Travelling with a dog

**B1 / 交通方式**

- **Claire:** Can I take my dog on the train to Bristol tomorrow?
- **Martin:** Yes, dogs are allowed, but they must stay on a lead.
- **Claire:** Does she need her own ticket?
- **Martin:** No. Just keep her off the seats and away from the aisle.

1. How must Claire keep her dog under control on the train?
   - A. With a muzzle / B. On a lead / C. In a crate
   - **答案：B** — 對話中的規定是狗隻必須繫上狗繩。
2. What does Martin say about a ticket for the dog?
   - A. No separate ticket is needed / B. A child ticket is needed / C. A first-class ticket is needed
   - **答案：A** — Martin 回答狗隻不需要另購車票。
3. Where should the dog not sit?
   - A. On the floor / B. Beside Claire / C. On the seats
   - **答案：C** — Martin 提醒她不要讓狗坐在座位上。

---

## b1-46 · A surprise picnic

**B1 / 特別場合**

- **Zara:** Our mum is turning sixty next month. Shall we organise a surprise picnic?
- **Harry:** Lovely idea. Her favourite place is the lake, but we need a plan if it rains.
- **Zara:** We can use my conservatory instead. I will ask everyone to keep the date free.
- **Harry:** I will make her favourite carrot cake and bring plates.

1. Whose birthday are they planning?
   - A. Their mother’s sixtieth / B. Their mother’s fiftieth / C. Their aunt’s sixtieth
   - **答案：A** — Zara 說「our mum」，並提到她下個月滿六十歲。
2. Where can the picnic move if it rains?
   - A. To a restaurant by the lake / B. To Harry’s office / C. To Zara’s conservatory
   - **答案：C** — 下雨的話可以改到 Zara 的陽光房。
3. What will Harry prepare for the celebration?
   - A. A fruit salad / B. A carrot cake / C. A chocolate cake
   - **答案：B** — Harry 會準備紅蘿蔔蛋糕。

---

## b1-47 · Graduation photographs

**B1 / 特別場合**

- **Isabel:** After the graduation ceremony, shall we take family photographs in the garden?
- **Noah:** The forecast says it may rain. The hall has a large window with good light.
- **Isabel:** Good thinking. The ceremony ends at eleven, so meet us there at quarter past.
- **Noah:** I will tell Auntie Rose. She is bringing her camera.

1. Where do they decide to take graduation photographs?
   - A. In the garden / B. Outside the station / C. In the hall
   - **答案：C** — 因為可能下雨，他們決定在大堂拍照。
2. When should the family meet for photographs?
   - A. At 11:45 / B. At 11:15 / C. At 11:00
   - **答案：B** — 典禮十一時結束，他們相約十一時十五分。
3. Who will bring a camera to graduation?
   - A. Auntie Rose / B. Noah / C. The ceremony organiser
   - **答案：A** — Rose 阿姨會帶相機。

---

## b1-48 · A retirement card

**B1 / 特別場合**

- **Paul:** We are collecting messages for Mr Patel’s retirement card. Have you written yours?
- **Jade:** Not yet. When do you need it?
- **Paul:** By Wednesday lunchtime, so I can give him the card at Friday’s lunch.
- **Jade:** I will write it this evening and leave it on your desk tomorrow.

1. What are colleagues preparing for Mr Patel?
   - A. A leaving speech competition / B. A retirement card / C. A birthday calendar
   - **答案：B** — 同事正在準備退休紀念卡。
2. What is the deadline for Jade’s message?
   - A. Wednesday lunchtime / B. Friday lunchtime / C. Tuesday evening
   - **答案：A** — Jade 最遲要在星期三午飯時間交上留言。
3. Where will Jade leave her message?
   - A. At the restaurant / B. In Mr Patel’s office / C. On Paul’s desk
   - **答案：C** — 她說明天會把留言放在 Paul 的書桌上。

---

## b1-49 · An anniversary meal

**B1 / 特別場合**

- **Grace:** I booked a table for our anniversary on the twelfth. Is seven o’clock convenient?
- **Hugh:** Yes. Did you choose the Italian restaurant near the theatre?
- **Grace:** I tried, but it was full. We have a table at the Indian restaurant by the river.
- **Hugh:** That sounds good. I have wanted to try their vegetarian dishes.

1. When is the anniversary table booked?
   - A. At seven on the twelfth / B. At seven on the twentieth / C. At eight on the twelfth
   - **答案：A** — 餐桌訂在十二日七時。
2. Why did Grace not book the Italian restaurant?
   - A. It was closed for repairs / B. It had no vegetarian food / C. It was full
   - **答案：C** — 意大利餐廳已經客滿。
3. What is Hugh interested in eating?
   - A. Italian desserts / B. Vegetarian dishes / C. Seafood dishes
   - **答案：B** — Hugh 想試素菜。

---

## b1-50 · Welcoming a new baby

**B1 / 特別場合**

- **Nadia:** We are visiting Emma and her new baby on Sunday. Shall we take flowers?
- **Simon:** Emma said she has plenty. A cooked meal might be more useful.
- **Nadia:** Good idea. I can make a vegetable lasagne on Saturday.
- **Simon:** I will bring it in a container she does not need to return.

1. When will they visit Emma?
   - A. On Saturday / B. On Monday / C. On Sunday
   - **答案：C** — 他們會在星期日探望 Emma。
2. Why do they choose a meal instead of flowers?
   - A. The baby dislikes flowers / B. Emma already has plenty of flowers / C. The flower shop is closed
   - **答案：B** — Emma 表示已經有很多花。
3. What will Nadia cook for the visit?
   - A. Vegetable lasagne / B. Chicken soup / C. Cheese sandwiches
   - **答案：A** — Nadia 會煮蔬菜千層麵。

---

## b1-51 · The community award

**B1 / 特別場合**

- **Olivia:** The volunteers’ award evening is next Thursday at the town hall.
- **Finlay:** I am pleased that Mrs Brown has been nominated. She runs the reading group.
- **Olivia:** Yes, and each nominee can bring two guests. Doors open at six thirty.
- **Finlay:** I hope her husband and daughter can come to support her.

1. Where will the awards evening take place?
   - A. At the school hall / B. At the town hall / C. At the library
   - **答案：B** — 頒獎晚會在市政廳舉行。
2. What does Mrs Brown do for the community?
   - A. She runs a reading group / B. She teaches swimming / C. She organises a football team
   - **答案：A** — Brown 太太負責讀書小組。
3. How many guests may each nominee bring?
   - A. One / B. Four / C. Two
   - **答案：C** — 每位獲提名者可帶兩位嘉賓。

---

## b1-52 · Choosing a matinee

**B1 / 娛樂**

- **Molly:** The new comedy is showing at half past two and eight tonight. Which suits you?
- **Arun:** The afternoon show, please. I promised to help my neighbour this evening.
- **Molly:** Fine. The cinema offers cheaper tickets before five, too.
- **Arun:** Great. I will book two seats near the back.

1. Which comedy screening will they attend?
   - A. The 2:30 screening / B. The 8:00 screening / C. The 5:00 screening
   - **答案：A** — Arun 選擇下午兩時半的場次。
2. Why is Arun busy this evening?
   - A. He is working at the cinema / B. He is visiting a theatre / C. He will help his neighbour
   - **答案：C** — Arun 答應今晚幫助鄰居。
3. Where will Arun book their seats?
   - A. Beside the screen / B. Near the back / C. In the front row
   - **答案：B** — 他會預訂靠後的座位。

---

## b1-53 · Library film club

**B1 / 娛樂**

- **Kate:** The library film club is showing a documentary about bees this Friday.
- **Isaac:** Do I need to be a library member to attend?
- **Kate:** No, anyone can come, but please reserve a place because the room is small.
- **Isaac:** I will do that online tonight. The subject sounds fascinating.

1. What is Friday’s documentary about?
   - A. Birds / B. Garden design / C. Bees
   - **答案：C** — 星期五的紀錄片介紹蜜蜂。
2. Who can attend the library film club?
   - A. Only local school pupils / B. Anyone who reserves a place / C. Only library members
   - **答案：B** — 任何人都可以參加，但要先預留座位。
3. Why should Isaac reserve a place?
   - A. The room is small / B. The film is expensive / C. The library closes early
   - **答案：A** — 由於房間細小，需要預留座位。

---

## b1-54 · The theatre interval

**B1 / 娛樂**

- **Freya:** The play starts at seven thirty and lasts about two hours, including an interval.
- **Oliver:** Shall we order drinks for the interval before the show begins?
- **Freya:** Yes. The queue can be long, and we will only have fifteen minutes.
- **Oliver:** I will order two teas while you find our seats.

1. What time does the play begin?
   - A. At 8:00 / B. At 7:30 / C. At 7:00
   - **答案：B** — 話劇七時半開始。
2. How long is the interval?
   - A. Fifteen minutes / B. Thirty minutes / C. Ten minutes
   - **答案：A** — 中場休息只有十五分鐘。
3. What will Oliver order before the performance?
   - A. Two coffees / B. Two glasses of water / C. Two teas
   - **答案：C** — Oliver 會先預訂兩杯茶。

---

## b1-55 · A puzzle evening

**B1 / 娛樂**

- **Tessa:** We are holding a puzzle evening at the youth centre. Would your family like to join?
- **Robert:** Yes, although our youngest is only eight. Is there anything suitable for her?
- **Tessa:** There is a table of easier puzzles, and families can work together.
- **Robert:** Perfect. We will arrive at six and bring a bottle of squash.

1. Where is the puzzle evening being held?
   - A. At the youth centre / B. At the swimming pool / C. At the village cinema
   - **答案：A** — 解謎活動在青年中心舉行。
2. What provision is there for younger children?
   - A. A separate film screening / B. A supervised swimming lesson / C. A table of easier puzzles
   - **答案：C** — 活動設有較簡單的謎題桌，適合年幼孩子。
3. What will Robert’s family bring?
   - A. A packet of biscuits / B. A bottle of squash / C. A box of puzzles
   - **答案：B** — Robert 一家會帶一瓶濃縮果汁飲品。

---

## b1-56 · Watching a radio drama recording

**B1 / 娛樂**

- **Maya:** The radio station is inviting local people to watch a drama being recorded on Thursday.
- **Callum:** That sounds fun. Does the audience have to stay completely silent?
- **Maya:** During the scenes, yes, but the producer will explain when to clap. Tickets are free, and you must arrive by six.
- **Callum:** I will book us two places. I would like to see how they make the sound effects.

1. What can local people watch at the radio station?
   - A. A singing competition / B. A live news report / C. A drama being recorded
   - **答案：C** — 電台邀請本地居民觀看廣播劇錄製。
2. When should the audience clap?
   - A. Throughout every scene / B. When the producer tells them to / C. Whenever an actor enters
   - **答案：B** — 演出場景錄製期間要保持安靜，製作人會說明何時鼓掌。
3. What particularly interests Callum about the recording?
   - A. How the sound effects are made / B. How the tickets are printed / C. How the news is written
   - **答案：A** — Callum 想看看音效是如何製作的。

---

## b1-57 · A comedy night change

**B1 / 娛樂**

- **Ruby:** The comedy night has moved from the upstairs room to the main hall.
- **Adil:** Does that affect our tickets?
- **Ruby:** No, all tickets are still valid. The doors now open at eight, half an hour later than planned.
- **Adil:** Thanks for checking. We can have dinner before we go.

1. Which room will host the comedy night?
   - A. The café / B. The main hall / C. The upstairs room
   - **答案：B** — 喜劇之夜改在主禮堂舉行。
2. What must ticket holders do about their tickets?
   - A. Keep using their existing tickets / B. Exchange them at the hall / C. Buy new tickets online
   - **答案：A** — 原有門票仍然有效。
3. When will the doors open now?
   - A. At 7:30 / B. At 8:30 / C. At 8:00
   - **答案：C** — 開門時間現改為八時。

## b1-58 · A choir rehearsal

**B1 / 音樂**

- **Ella:** The choir rehearsal is in the church hall this week, not the music room.
- **Samir:** Thanks. Is it still on Tuesday evening?
- **Ella:** Yes, at seven. Please bring the blue songbook because we are practising the summer concert pieces.
- **Samir:** I will put it in my bag now so I do not forget.

1. Where is this week’s choir rehearsal?
   - A. In the church hall / B. In the music room / C. In the concert theatre
   - **答案：A** — 本星期的合唱團排練在教堂禮堂。
2. When will the choir rehearse?
   - A. Thursday at seven / B. Tuesday at six / C. Tuesday at seven
   - **答案：C** — 排練仍在星期二晚上七時。
3. Which book should Samir bring?
   - A. The concert programme / B. The blue songbook / C. The red notebook
   - **答案：B** — 他需要帶藍色歌集。

---

## b1-59 · Learning the violin

**B1 / 音樂**

- **Phoebe:** How are your violin lessons going?
- **Dev:** Better now. At first I held the bow too tightly, but my teacher showed me how to relax my hand.
- **Phoebe:** How often do you practise?
- **Dev:** For twenty minutes most mornings, before I leave for college.

1. What did Dev initially do with the bow?
   - A. He kept dropping it / B. He moved it too slowly / C. He held it too tightly
   - **答案：C** — Dev 起初把琴弓握得太緊。
2. Who helped Dev relax his hand?
   - A. His sister / B. His violin teacher / C. His college friend
   - **答案：B** — 小提琴老師教他放鬆手部。
3. When does Dev usually practise?
   - A. Most mornings before college / B. Every evening after dinner / C. Only on Saturday afternoons
   - **答案：A** — 他通常在上學前的早上練習。

---

## b1-60 · The park bandstand

**B1 / 音樂**

- **Iris:** The brass band is playing at the park bandstand on Sunday afternoon.
- **Dylan:** Is there somewhere to sit? My grandfather would like to come.
- **Iris:** There are a few benches, but it is sensible to bring folding chairs.
- **Dylan:** I will pack two chairs and a blanket in case it is chilly.

1. Which group will perform in the park?
   - A. A school choir / B. A brass band / C. A string quartet
   - **答案：B** — 銅管樂隊會在公園演出。
2. What seating does Iris recommend bringing?
   - A. Folding chairs / B. A picnic table / C. A wooden bench
   - **答案：A** — Iris 建議帶摺椅。
3. Why will Dylan also pack a blanket?
   - A. Rain is certain / B. The benches are wet / C. It might be chilly
   - **答案：C** — 他擔心天氣可能有點冷。

---

## b1-61 · Choosing a concert seat

**B1 / 音樂**

- **Harriet:** There are tickets left for the folk concert, either near the stage or by the exit.
- **Leo:** I would prefer the exit. My hearing is sensitive, and the speakers may be loud near the stage.
- **Harriet:** All right. Those seats are also five pounds cheaper.
- **Leo:** Excellent. Could you book them while I check the train times?

1. Where would Leo rather sit?
   - A. By the exit / B. Near the stage / C. In the front row
   - **答案：A** — Leo 較想坐在出口附近。
2. Why does Leo want to avoid the stage area?
   - A. He cannot see the performers / B. He needs space for a wheelchair / C. The speakers may be too loud
   - **答案：C** — Leo 聽覺敏感，舞台附近的揚聲器可能太大聲。
3. What will Leo check while Harriet books?
   - A. The weather forecast / B. Train times / C. The concert menu
   - **答案：B** — Harriet 訂票時，Leo 會查看火車時間。

---

## b1-62 · A song for the assembly

**B1 / 音樂**

- **Sana:** The school assembly needs a song about friendship. Have you chosen one?
- **Matthew:** Yes, but the original is too high for some pupils to sing comfortably.
- **Sana:** Could the pianist play it in a lower key?
- **Matthew:** She has agreed to do that. We will practise it on Wednesday.

1. What theme should the assembly song have?
   - A. Travel / B. The environment / C. Friendship
   - **答案：C** — 學校早會需要一首關於友誼的歌。
2. What difficulty does the original song present?
   - A. It is too short for the assembly / B. Its notes are too high for some pupils / C. Its words are in another language
   - **答案：B** — 原曲音調太高，部分學生唱得不舒服。
3. How will the pianist help the pupils?
   - A. Play the song in a lower key / B. Write a completely new song / C. Sing the song alone
   - **答案：A** — 鋼琴伴奏者會用較低的調演奏。

---

## b1-63 · Borrowing a keyboard

**B1 / 音樂**

- **Rosie:** Could I borrow your keyboard for the community concert next month?
- **Darren:** Yes, but one of the keys sticks occasionally.
- **Rosie:** That should be fine for our rehearsal. Do you have a stand for it too?
- **Darren:** I do. I can bring both to the village hall on Thursday.

1. Why does Rosie want to borrow a keyboard?
   - A. For a birthday meal / B. For a community concert / C. For a school examination
   - **答案：B** — Rosie 想借電子琴作社區音樂會之用。
2. What problem does the keyboard sometimes have?
   - A. One key sticks / B. The speakers produce no sound / C. The power cable is missing
   - **答案：A** — Darren 說其中一個琴鍵偶爾會卡住。
3. When will Darren bring the keyboard and stand?
   - A. On Tuesday / B. On Saturday / C. On Thursday
   - **答案：C** — Darren 會在星期四把電子琴及琴架帶到村禮堂。

---

## b1-64 · Trying an indoor climbing wall

**B1 / 近期個人經歷**

- **Alfie:** How was your first session at the indoor climbing centre?
- **Leah:** Better than expected. I was nervous about the height, but the instructor showed me how to use the safety rope.
- **Alfie:** Did you reach the top of the wall?
- **Leah:** No, only halfway. I am pleased I tried, though, and I have booked another session for next Saturday.

1. What made Leah nervous before climbing?
   - A. The height of the wall / B. The number of people watching / C. The cost of the equipment
   - **答案：A** — Leah 說自己起初擔心高度。
2. Who explained the safety rope to Leah?
   - A. Her brother / B. Another beginner / C. The instructor
   - **答案：C** — 導師向 Leah 示範如何使用安全繩。
3. How does Leah feel about the session?
   - A. Certain she needs no more practice / B. Glad she tried despite not reaching the top / C. Disappointed and unwilling to return
   - **答案：B** — 她雖然只爬到一半，仍慶幸有嘗試，並已預訂下星期六再去。

---

## b1-65 · A first volunteering day

**B1 / 近期個人經歷**

- **Liam:** You volunteered at the animal shelter yesterday, did you not?
- **Zoe:** Yes. I expected to walk dogs, but I spent the morning preparing food for the cats.
- **Liam:** Would you go back?
- **Zoe:** Definitely. The staff were welcoming, and next time they will show me how to care for rabbits.

1. Where did Zoe volunteer yesterday?
   - A. At a community garden / B. At a children’s hospital / C. At an animal shelter
   - **答案：C** — Zoe 昨天在動物收容所做義工。
2. What did Zoe actually do in the morning?
   - A. Cleaned a rabbit enclosure / B. Prepared food for cats / C. Walked dogs
   - **答案：B** — 她早上為貓準備食物。
3. What will Zoe learn about on her next visit?
   - A. Caring for rabbits / B. Training dogs / C. Feeding garden birds
   - **答案：A** — 下次職員會教她照顧兔子。

## b1-66 · A weekend at the orchard

**B1 / 近期個人經歷**

- **Ava:** Did you enjoy your weekend at your cousin’s farm?
- **Ravi:** Yes. We picked apples on Saturday, though I had expected to help with the sheep.
- **Ava:** What happened to all the apples you picked?
- **Ravi:** A local café bought most of them, and my cousin kept a basket for us to bake a pie.

1. What did Ravi do at the farm on Saturday?
   - A. Repaired a fence / B. Picked apples / C. Looked after sheep
   - **答案：B** — Ravi 原以為會幫忙照顧羊，實際上卻去摘蘋果。
2. Who bought most of the apples?
   - A. A local café / B. A large supermarket / C. A school kitchen
   - **答案：A** — 大部分蘋果賣給了一間本地咖啡店。
3. Why did Ravi’s cousin keep a basket of apples?
   - A. To feed the sheep / B. To plant new trees / C. To bake a pie with Ravi
   - **答案：C** — 留起的那籃蘋果是用來與 Ravi 一起焗批。

## b1-67 · Finishing a long book

**B1 / 近期個人經歷**

- **Chloe:** I finally finished that history book you lent me.
- **Peter:** What did you think of it?
- **Chloe:** The first chapters were slow, but the stories about ordinary families became fascinating.
- **Peter:** I am glad you liked those. You can pass it to Lucy when you see her.

1. What has Chloe just finished reading?
   - A. A history book / B. A crime novel / C. A travel guide
   - **答案：A** — Chloe 終於讀完那本歷史書。
2. Which part of the book did Chloe find fascinating?
   - A. The opening chapters / B. A list of famous battles / C. Stories about ordinary families
   - **答案：C** — 她覺得書中普通家庭的故事很吸引。
3. Who should receive the book next?
   - A. Chloe’s teacher / B. Lucy / C. Peter’s brother
   - **答案：B** — Peter 說可以把書交給 Lucy。

## b1-68 · A cooking course discovery

**B1 / 近期個人經歷**

- **Elliot:** I went on a vegetarian cooking course last weekend.
- **Mina:** Did you learn anything you will make again?
- **Elliot:** Yes, a lentil soup with lemon. I was surprised how much flavour the lemon added.
- **Mina:** Could you send me the recipe? I have lentils at home.

1. What kind of course did Elliot attend?
   - A. A bread-making course / B. A photography course / C. A vegetarian cooking course
   - **答案：C** — Elliot 上週末參加了素食烹飪課。
2. Which ingredient surprised Elliot in the soup?
   - A. Cheese / B. Lemon / C. Potato
   - **答案：B** — 他沒想到檸檬能為湯增添那麼多味道。
3. Why does Mina ask for the recipe?
   - A. She has lentils at home / B. She is teaching the course / C. She dislikes lemon
   - **答案：A** — Mina 家中有扁豆，所以想要食譜。

## b1-69 · Sharing a garden

**B1 / 自選主題討論**

- **Imogen:** Would a shared garden improve our street?
- **Khalid:** I think so. People could grow vegetables and get to know their neighbours.
- **Imogen:** I agree, although someone would need to organise watering during holidays.
- **Khalid:** Perhaps residents could make a rota, so nobody has to do it every day.

1. What benefit of a shared garden does Khalid mention?
   - A. Residents would no longer need shops / B. Neighbours could get to know one another / C. Every house would get free tools
   - **答案：B** — Khalid 認為共享花園能讓鄰居互相認識。
2. What practical difficulty does Imogen raise?
   - A. Watering plants during holidays / B. Finding a gardening teacher / C. Keeping the garden open at night
   - **答案：A** — Imogen 提出假期期間澆水的問題。
3. How might residents share the watering?
   - A. By hiring a full-time gardener / B. By watering only once a year / C. By making a rota
   - **答案：C** — Khalid 建議居民編排輪值表。

## b1-70 · Phones at mealtimes

**B1 / 自選主題討論**

- **Alice:** Do you think families should put phones away at dinner?
- **Bilal:** Usually, yes. It helps people listen to one another.
- **Alice:** I agree, but I would make an exception if someone was expecting an urgent call.
- **Bilal:** That seems fair. The rule should help conversation, not cause worry.

1. Why does Bilal support putting phones away?
   - A. It helps people listen to each other / B. It saves electricity / C. It makes dinner quicker
   - **答案：A** — Bilal 認為這樣有助大家互相聆聽。
2. What exception does Alice suggest?
   - A. A new online game / B. An ordinary shopping message / C. An expected urgent call
   - **答案：C** — Alice 認為等待緊急電話時可以例外。
3. How does Bilal respond to Alice’s exception?
   - A. He changes the subject / B. He thinks it is fair / C. He strongly disagrees
   - **答案：B** — Bilal 說這個例外合理。

## b1-71 · A later school start

**B1 / 自選主題討論**

- **Eva:** Some people want school to start later. What do you think?
- **Nathan:** Teenagers might get more sleep, but a later finish could make after-school clubs difficult.
- **Eva:** That is true. Perhaps the timetable could include shorter breaks.
- **Nathan:** Possibly, although pupils still need enough time to eat and rest.

1. What possible advantage of a later start does Nathan mention?
   - A. Lessons would become optional / B. The school would need fewer teachers / C. Teenagers might sleep more
   - **答案：C** — Nathan 說青少年可能睡得多一點。
2. What concern does Nathan have about a later finish?
   - A. It would stop pupils having breakfast / B. It could affect after-school clubs / C. It would close the library
   - **答案：B** — 他擔心放學較遲會影響課外活動。
3. What does Eva suggest changing in the timetable?
   - A. The length of breaks / B. The number of school subjects / C. The school holidays
   - **答案：A** — Eva 建議縮短小息時間。

## b1-72 · Buying second-hand

**B1 / 自選主題討論**

- **Lauren:** I have started buying second-hand clothes. It often saves money.
- **Yusuf:** I do that too, and it means useful clothes stay in use longer.
- **Lauren:** The only problem is finding the right size.
- **Yusuf:** True. I check the measurements before ordering online.

1. What financial advantage does Lauren mention?
   - A. Delivery is always free / B. Second-hand clothes often cost less / C. Shops pay customers to take clothes
   - **答案：B** — Lauren 說購買二手衣服通常可以省錢。
2. What difficulty does Lauren experience?
   - A. Finding the right size / B. Paying for new clothes / C. Repairing washing machines
   - **答案：A** — 她覺得難以找到合適尺碼。
3. How does Yusuf reduce the sizing problem online?
   - A. He orders every size / B. He avoids reading descriptions / C. He checks the measurements
   - **答案：C** — Yusuf 會在網購前查看尺寸數據。

## b1-73 · Libraries beyond books

**B1 / 自選主題討論**

- **Amy:** Should libraries offer more than books?
- **Rohan:** Yes. Ours lends tools, which is useful when you only need something once.
- **Amy:** That sounds helpful, especially for people with little storage space.
- **Rohan:** Exactly. The library could also run short repair workshops.

1. What unusual items does Rohan’s library lend?
   - A. Tools / B. Bicycles / C. Musical instruments
   - **答案：A** — Rohan 的圖書館也借出工具。
2. Who might particularly benefit from borrowing items?
   - A. People who own large workshops / B. People who never repair anything / C. People with little storage space
   - **答案：C** — Amy 認為儲物空間少的人特別受惠。
3. What additional activity does Rohan propose?
   - A. A permanent café / B. Short repair workshops / C. Weekly football matches
   - **答案：B** — Rohan 建議舉辦簡短的維修工作坊。

## b1-74 · Clarifying a question

**B1 / 理解提問**

- **Examiner:** Tell me about a place you enjoy visiting in your area.
- **Candidate:** Do you mean a place outdoors, or can it be indoors too?
- **Examiner:** Either is fine. Explain why you enjoy going there.
- **Candidate:** In that case, I will talk about the local art gallery because its exhibitions change regularly.

1. What does the examiner ask the candidate to describe?
   - A. A favourite school subject / B. A planned holiday abroad / C. An enjoyable local place
   - **答案：C** — 考官請考生描述喜歡去的本地地方。
2. What does the candidate ask the examiner to clarify?
   - A. Whether photographs are allowed / B. Whether the place may be indoors / C. How long the answer should be
   - **答案：B** — 考生想確認地方是否可以在室內。
3. Why does the candidate choose the art gallery?
   - A. Its exhibitions change regularly / B. It is always completely empty / C. It sells inexpensive paintings
   - **答案：A** — 考生喜歡美術館經常更換展覽。
